// ==========================================
// CONFIGURACIÓN
// ==========================================

const BACKEND_URL =
    "https://jonis-backend.onrender.com";


// ==========================================
// ELEMENTOS HTML
// ==========================================

const messagesDiv =
    document.getElementById("messages");

const userInput =
    document.getElementById("user-input");

const sendBtn =
    document.getElementById("send-btn");

const imageInput =
    document.getElementById("image-input");

const imagePreview =
    document.getElementById("image-preview");

const imagePreviewContainer =
    document.getElementById(
        "image-preview-container"
    );

const removeImageBtn =
    document.getElementById("remove-image");

const fileInput =
    document.getElementById("file-input");

const modelSelect =
    document.getElementById("model-select");

// ==========================================
// GUARDAR MODELO SELECCIONADO
// ==========================================

const savedModel =
    localStorage.getItem("jonisai_model");

if (savedModel) {
    modelSelect.value = savedModel;
}

modelSelect.addEventListener(
    "change",
    function() {

        localStorage.setItem(
            "jonisai_model",
            modelSelect.value
        );

    }
);

// ==========================================
// CARGAR MODELOS
// ==========================================

async function loadModels() {

    try {

        const response =
            await fetch(
                `${BACKEND_URL}/models`
            );

        const data =
            await response.json();

        modelSelect.innerHTML = "";

        data.models.forEach(function(model) {

            const option =
                document.createElement("option");

            option.value =
                model.key;

            option.textContent =
                model.name;

            if (
                model.key === data.default
            ) {

                option.selected = true;

            }

            modelSelect.appendChild(option);

        });

    }

    catch (error) {

        console.error(
            "Error cargando modelos:",
            error
        );

        modelSelect.innerHTML =
            '<option value="">❌ Error cargando modelos</option>';

    }

}

const filePreview =
    document.getElementById("file-preview");

const fileName =
    document.getElementById("file-name");

const removeFileBtn =
    document.getElementById("remove-file");


// ==========================================
// ESTADO
// ==========================================

let selectedImage = null;

let selectedFile = null;

let isSending = false;

// ==========================================
// SISTEMA DE CHATS
// ==========================================

const sidebar =
    document.getElementById("sidebar");

const openSidebarBtn =
    document.getElementById("open-sidebar-btn");

const closeSidebarBtn =
    document.getElementById("close-sidebar-btn");

const newChatBtn =
    document.getElementById("new-chat-btn");

const conversationList =
    document.getElementById("conversation-list");


// ==========================================
// ABRIR / CERRAR SIDEBAR
// ==========================================

if (openSidebarBtn) {

    openSidebarBtn.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function() {

            sidebar.classList.add(
                "sidebar-open"
            );

        }
    );

}


if (closeSidebarBtn) {

    closeSidebarBtn.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function() {

            sidebar.classList.remove(
                "sidebar-open"
            );

        }
    );

}


// ==========================================
// CERRAR MENU AL TOCAR FUERA
document.addEventListener("click", function(event) {
    if (!sidebar || !sidebar.classList.contains("sidebar-open")) {
        return;
    }

    if (
        !sidebar.contains(event.target) &&
        openSidebarBtn &&
        !openSidebarBtn.contains(event.target)
    ) {
        sidebar.classList.remove("sidebar-open");
    }
});

// CARGAR LISTA DE CONVERSACIONES
// ==========================================

async function loadConversations() {

    try {

        conversationList.innerHTML =
            '<div class="history-loading">Cargando chats...</div>';


        const response =
            await fetch(
                `${BACKEND_URL}/conversations`
            );


        const data =
            await response.json();


        if (!response.ok) {

            throw new Error(
                data.error ||
                "No se pudieron cargar los chats."
            );

        }


        conversationList.innerHTML = "";


        const conversations =
            data.conversations ||
            data ||
            [];


        if (
            conversations.length === 0
        ) {

            conversationList.innerHTML =
                '<div class="history-empty">No hay chats todavía.</div>';

            return;

        }


        conversations.forEach(
            function(conversation) {

                createConversationItem(
                    conversation
                );

            }
        );


    }

    catch (error) {

        console.error(
            "Error cargando conversaciones:",
            error
        );


        conversationList.innerHTML =
    `<div class="history-error">❌ Error: ${error.message}</div>`;

    }

}


// ==========================================
// CREAR ELEMENTO DE CHAT
// ==========================================

function createConversationItem(
    conversation
) {

    const item =
        document.createElement(
            "div"
        );


    item.className =
        "conversation-item";

    const id =
        conversation.id;

    item.dataset.id =
        id;


    const title =
        conversation.title ||
        conversation.name ||
        `Chat ${id}`;


    item.innerHTML = `

        <button
            type="button"
            class="conversation-button"
        >

            <span class="conversation-icon">
                💬
            </span>

            <span class="conversation-title">
                ${escapeHtml(title)}
            </span>

        </button>

        <button
            type="button"
            class="delete-conversation-btn"
            title="Eliminar chat"
        >
            🗑️
        </button>

    `;


    const conversationButton =
        item.querySelector(
            ".conversation-button"
        );


    const deleteButton =
        item.querySelector(
            ".delete-conversation-btn"
        );


    // Abrir conversación

    conversationButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function() {

            openConversation(
                id
            );

        }
    );


    // Eliminar conversación

    deleteButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function(event) {

            event.stopPropagation();

            deleteConversation(
                id
            );

        }
    );


    if (
        String(id) ===
        String(currentConversationId)
    ) {

        item.classList.add(
            "active"
        );

    }


    conversationList.appendChild(
        item
    );

}


// ==========================================
// ABRIR CHAT
// ==========================================

async function openConversation(
    conversationId
) {

    try {

        const response =
            await fetch(
                `${BACKEND_URL}/conversations/${conversationId}`
            );


        const data =
            await response.json();


        if (!response.ok) {

            throw new Error(
                data.error ||
                "La conversación no existe."
            );

        }


        currentConversationId =
            conversationId;


        localStorage.setItem(
            "jonisai_conversation_id",
            conversationId
        );


        messagesDiv.innerHTML = "";


        const messages =
            data.messages ||
            data.conversation?.messages ||
            [];


        messages.forEach(
            function(message) {

                if (
                    message.role === "user" ||
                    message.role === "assistant"
                ) {

                    addMessage(
                        message.role,
                        message.content
                    );

                }

            }
        );


        conversationHistory =
            messages.filter(
                function(message) {

                    return (
                        message.role === "user" ||
                        message.role === "assistant"
                    );

                }
            ).map(
                function(message) {

                    return {

                        role:
                            message.role,

                        content:
                            message.content

                    };

                }
            );


        localStorage.setItem(
            "jonisai_history",
            JSON.stringify(
                conversationHistory
            )
        );


        document
            .querySelectorAll(
                ".conversation-item"
            )
            .forEach(
                function(item) {

                    item.classList.remove(
                        "active"
                    );

                }
            );


        const activeItem =
            Array.from(
                document.querySelectorAll(
                    ".conversation-item"
                )
            ).find(
                function(item) {

                    const button =
                        item.querySelector(
                            ".conversation-button"
                        );

                    return button &&
                        item.dataset.id ===
                        String(conversationId);

                }
            );


        if (activeItem) {

            activeItem.classList.add(
                "active"
            );

        }


        // En teléfono cerramos el menú

        sidebar.classList.remove(
            "sidebar-open"
        );


        scrollToBottom();


    }

    catch (error) {

        console.error(
            "Error abriendo conversación:",
            error
        );


        alert(
            "❌ " +
            error.message
        );

    }

}


// ==========================================
// ELIMINAR CHAT
// ==========================================

async function deleteConversation(
    conversationId
) {

    const confirmed =
        confirm(
            "¿Quieres eliminar este chat?"
        );


    if (!confirmed) {

        return;

    }


    try {

        const response =
            await fetch(
                `${BACKEND_URL}/conversations/${conversationId}`,
                {
                    method:
                        "DELETE"
                }
            );


        const data =
            await response.json();


        if (!response.ok) {

            throw new Error(
                data.error ||
                "No se pudo eliminar el chat."
            );

        }


        if (
            String(conversationId) ===
            String(currentConversationId)
        ) {

            currentConversationId =
                null;


            conversationHistory =
                [];


            localStorage.removeItem(
                "jonisai_conversation_id"
            );


            localStorage.removeItem(
                "jonisai_history"
            );


            messagesDiv.innerHTML = "";


            showWelcomeMessage();

        }


        await loadConversations();


    }

    catch (error) {

        console.error(
            "Error eliminando conversación:",
            error
        );


        alert(
            "❌ " +
            error.message
        );

    }

}


// ==========================================
// NUEVO CHAT
// ==========================================

async function createNewChat() {

    try {

        const response =
            await fetch(
                `${BACKEND_URL}/conversations`,
                {
                    method:
                        "POST",

                    headers: {
                        "Content-Type":
                            "application/json"
                    },

                    body:
                        JSON.stringify({
                            model:
                                modelSelect.value
                        })
                }
            );


        const data =
            await response.json();


        if (!response.ok) {

            throw new Error(
                data.error ||
                "No se pudo crear el chat."
            );

        }


        const newId =
            data.id ||
            data.conversation_id ||
            data.conversation?.id;


        if (!newId) {

            throw new Error(
                "El servidor no devolvió el ID del nuevo chat."
            );

        }


        currentConversationId =
            newId;


        conversationHistory =
            [];


        localStorage.setItem(
            "jonisai_conversation_id",
            newId
        );


        localStorage.setItem(
            "jonisai_history",
            JSON.stringify([])
        );


        messagesDiv.innerHTML = "";


        showWelcomeMessage();


        await loadConversations();


        sidebar.classList.remove(
            "sidebar-open"
        );


        userInput.focus();


    }

    catch (error) {

        console.error(
            "Error creando chat:",
            error
        );


        alert(
            "❌ " +
            error.message
        );

    }

}

    addMessage(
        "assistant",
        `¡Hola! 👋 Soy JonisAI 🤖

¿Qué quieres hacer el día de hoy?

Puedo ayudarte con preguntas, ideas, aprendizaje, programación, textos, problemas y proyectos.

Puedes escribirme lo que necesites y te ayudaré paso a paso.`
    );

}

if (newChatBtn) {

// ==========================================
// MEMORIA DE LA CONVERSACIÓN
// ==========================================
//
// Aquí guardamos los mensajes que ya
// tuvieron respuesta.
//
// Ejemplo:
//
// {
//     role: "user",
//     content: "hola"
// }
//
// {
//     role: "assistant",
//     content: "¡Hola!"
// }
//
// Luego enviamos esta información
// al backend como "history".
//

let conversationHistory =
    JSON.parse(
        localStorage.getItem(
            "jonisai_history"
        )
    ) || [];
let currentConversationId =
    localStorage.getItem(
        "jonisai_conversation_id"
    );


// ==========================================
// SCROLL
// ==========================================

function scrollToBottom() {

    messagesDiv.scrollTo({

        top:
            messagesDiv.scrollHeight,

        behavior:
            "smooth"

    });

}


// ==========================================
// ESCAPAR HTML
// ==========================================

function escapeHtml(text) {

    const div =
        document.createElement("div");

    div.textContent =
        text;

    return div.innerHTML;

}


// ==========================================
// CONVERTIR URLS EN ENLACES
// ==========================================

function linkify(text) {

    const urlRegex =
        /(https?:\/\/[^\s<]+)/g;

    return text.replace(

        urlRegex,

        function(url) {

            return `<a
                href="${url}"
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                class="chat-link"
            >${url}</a>`;

        }

    );

}


// ==========================================
// FORMATEAR TEXTO
// ==========================================

function formatText(text) {

    let safeText =
        escapeHtml(
            String(text || "")
        );

    safeText =
        linkify(
            safeText
        );

    safeText =
        safeText.replace(
            /\n/g,
            "<br>"
        );

    return safeText;

}


// ==========================================
// FORMATEAR RESPUESTA
// ==========================================

function formatResponse(text) {
    const originalText = String(text || "");

    const codeRegex = /```([a-zA-Z0-9_+#.-]*)\n?([\s\S]*?)```/g;

    function formatInline(value) {
        let html = escapeHtml(value);

        html = html.replace(
            /`([^`\n]+)`/g,
            '<span class="inline-code">$1</span>'
        );

        html = html.replace(
            /\*\*([^*\n]+)\*\*/g,
            "<strong>$1</strong>"
        );

        html = html.replace(
            /(https?:\/\/[^\s<]+)/g,
            function(url) {
                return `<a href="${url}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="chat-link">${url}</a>`;
            }
        );

        return html;
    }

    function formatNormalText(value) {
        const lines = value.split("\n");

        let html = "";
        let paragraph = [];
        let unorderedList = false;
        let orderedList = false;

        function closeLists() {
            if (unorderedList) {
                html += "</ul>";
                unorderedList = false;
            }

            if (orderedList) {
                html += "</ol>";
                orderedList = false;
            }
        }

        function closeParagraph() {
            if (paragraph.length > 0) {
                const content = paragraph.join(" ").trim();

                if (content) {
                    html += `<div class="response-paragraph">${formatInline(content)}</div>`;
                }

                paragraph = [];
            }
        }

        for (const line of lines) {
            const trimmed = line.trim();

            if (!trimmed) {
                closeParagraph();
                closeLists();
                continue;
            }

            const heading = trimmed.match(/^#{1,3}\s+(.+)$/);

            if (heading) {
                closeParagraph();
                closeLists();

                html += `<div class="response-heading">${formatInline(heading[1])}</div>`;

                continue;
            }

            const unordered = trimmed.match(/^[-*]\s+(.+)$/);

            if (unordered) {
                closeParagraph();

                if (orderedList) {
                    html += "</ol>";
                    orderedList = false;
                }

                if (!unorderedList) {
                    html += '<ul class="response-list">';
                    unorderedList = true;
                }

                html += `<li>${formatInline(unordered[1])}</li>`;

                continue;
            }

            const ordered = trimmed.match(/^\d+[.)]\s+(.+)$/);

            if (ordered) {
                closeParagraph();

                if (unorderedList) {
                    html += "</ul>";
                    unorderedList = false;
                }

                if (!orderedList) {
                    html += '<ol class="response-list response-list-ordered">';
                    orderedList = true;
                }

                html += `<li>${formatInline(ordered[1])}</li>`;

                continue;
            }

            closeLists();

            paragraph.push(trimmed);
        }

        closeParagraph();
        closeLists();

        return html;
    }

    let result = "";
    let lastIndex = 0;
    let match;

    while ((match = codeRegex.exec(originalText)) !== null) {
        const textBefore = originalText.slice(lastIndex, match.index);

        result += formatNormalText(textBefore);

        const language = match[1] || "Código";
        const code = escapeHtml(match[2]);

        result += `
<div class="code-block">
    <div class="code-header">
        <span>${escapeHtml(language)}</span>
        <button class="copy-code-btn" type="button">📋 Copiar</button>
    </div>
    <pre><code>${code}</code></pre>
</div>`;

        lastIndex = codeRegex.lastIndex;
    }

    result += formatNormalText(
        originalText.slice(lastIndex)
    );

    return result;
}

// ==========================================
// COPIAR TEXTO
// ==========================================

async function copyText(text) {

if (

    navigator.clipboard &&

    window.isSecureContext

) {

    await navigator
        .clipboard
        .writeText(
            text
        );

    return;

}


const textarea =
    document.createElement(
        "textarea"
    );

textarea.value =
    text;

textarea.style.position =
    "fixed";

textarea.style.opacity =
    "0";

document.body.appendChild(
    textarea
);

textarea.select();

document.execCommand(
    "copy"
);

document.body.removeChild(
    textarea
);

}

// ==========================================
// BOTONES COPIAR
// ==========================================

function setupCopyButtons(container) {

const copyButtons =
    container.querySelectorAll(
        ".copy-code-btn"
    );


copyButtons.forEach(

    function(button) {

        button.addEventListener(

            "click",

            async function() {

                const codeBlock =
                    button.closest(
                        ".code-block"
                    );


                const codeElement =
                    codeBlock.querySelector(
                        "code"
                    );


                const code =
                    codeElement.textContent;


                const oldText =
                    button.textContent;


                try {

                    await copyText(
                        code
                    );


                    button.textContent =
                        "✓ Copiado";


                    setTimeout(

                        function() {

                            button.textContent =
                                oldText;

                        },

                        2000

                    );

                }

                catch (error) {

                    console.error(
                        "Error al copiar:",
                        error
                    );


                    button.textContent =
                        "❌ Error";


                    setTimeout(

                        function() {

                            button.textContent =
                                oldText;

                        },

                        2000

                    );

                }

            }

        );

    }

);

}

// ==========================================
// MOSTRAR MENSAJE
// ==========================================

function addMessage(
    role,
    content,
    rawHTML = false
) {

    const messageDiv =
        document.createElement(
            "div"
        );


    messageDiv.classList.add(
        "message",
        role
    );


    if (
        role === "assistant"
    ) {

        if (rawHTML) {

            messageDiv.innerHTML =
                content;

        }

        else {

            messageDiv.innerHTML =
                formatResponse(
                    content
                );

            setupCopyButtons(
                messageDiv
            );

        }

    }

    else {

        messageDiv.textContent =
            content;

    }


    messagesDiv.appendChild(
        messageDiv
    );


    scrollToBottom();


    return messageDiv;

}

// ==========================================
// MOSTRAR IMAGEN
// ==========================================

function addImageMessage(
imageData
) {

const imageMessage =
    document.createElement(
        "div"
    );


imageMessage.classList.add(
    "message",
    "user"
);


const image =
    document.createElement(
        "img"
    );


image.src =
    imageData;


image.alt =
    "Imagen enviada";


image.classList.add(
    "chat-image"
);


imageMessage.appendChild(
    image
);


messagesDiv.appendChild(
    imageMessage
);


scrollToBottom();

}

// ==========================================
// SELECCIONAR IMAGEN
// ==========================================

imageInput.addEventListener(

"change",

function() {

    const file =
        imageInput.files[0];


    if (!file) {

        return;

    }


    if (

        !file.type.startsWith(
            "image/"
        )

    ) {

        alert(
            "Selecciona una imagen válida."
        );


        imageInput.value =
            "";


        return;

    }


    const reader =
        new FileReader();


    reader.onload =
        function(event) {

            selectedImage =
                event.target.result;


            imagePreview.src =
                selectedImage;


            imagePreviewContainer.style.display =
                "flex";

        };


    reader.onerror =
        function() {

            alert(
                "No se pudo leer la imagen."
            );

        };


    reader.readAsDataURL(
        file
    );

}

);

        "";

    imagePreviewContainer.style.display =
        "none";

}

);

}

);

// ==========================================
// CONSULTAR BACKEND
// ==========================================

async function getResponse(
    userMessage,
    image
) {

    const url =
        `${BACKEND_URL}/chat`;

    console.log(
        "Enviando mensaje:",
        userMessage
    );

    console.log(
        "Modelo:",
        modelSelect.value
    );

    /*
       Creamos un controlador para evitar
       que una petición quede esperando
       indefinidamente.
    */

    const controller =
        new AbortController();

    const timeout =
        setTimeout(
            function() {
                controller.abort();
            },
            90000
        );

    let response;

    try {

        response =
            await fetch(
                url,
                {
                    method:
                        "POST",

                    headers: {
                        "Content-Type":
                            "application/json"
                    },

                    body:
                        JSON.stringify({
                            message:
                                userMessage,

                            image:
                                image,

                            model:
                                modelSelect.value,

                            conversation_id:
                                currentConversationId,

                            device_id:
                                localStorage.getItem(
                                    "jonisai_ultra_device_id"
                                ),

                            access_id:
                                localStorage.getItem(
                                    "jonisai_ultra_access_id"
                                )	
                        }),

                    signal:
                        controller.signal
                }
            );

    } catch (error) {

        if (
            error.name ===
            "AbortError"
        ) {

            throw new Error(
                "El servidor tardó demasiado en responder. Intenta nuevamente."
            );

        }

        throw new Error(
            "No se pudo conectar con JonisAI. Comprueba la conexión e inténtalo nuevamente."
        );

    } finally {

        clearTimeout(
            timeout
        );

    }

    let data = null;

    const contentType =
        response.headers.get(
            "content-type"
        ) || "";

    if (
        contentType.includes(
            "application/json"
        )
    ) {

        try {

            data =
                await response.json();

        } catch (error) {

            throw new Error(
                "El servidor devolvió una respuesta JSON inválida."
            );

        }

    } else {

        const text =
            await response.text();

        console.error(
            "Respuesta no JSON:",
            text
        );

        throw new Error(
            `El servidor respondió con un error (${response.status}).`
        );

    }

    if (
        !response.ok
    ) {

        console.error(
            "Error del backend:",
            data
        );

        throw new Error(
            data?.error ||
            data?.message ||
            `El servidor respondió ${response.status}.`
        );

    }

    if (
        data &&
        data.conversation_id
    ) {

        currentConversationId =
            data.conversation_id;

        localStorage.setItem(
            "jonisai_conversation_id",
            currentConversationId
        );

    }

    if (
        data &&
        typeof data.response ===
            "string" &&
        data.response.trim()
    ) {

        return data.response;

    }

    console.error(
        "Respuesta recibida sin contenido:",
        data
    );

    throw new Error(
        "JonisAI no recibió contenido del modelo."
    );

}

async function getStreamingTest(
    userMessage,
    messageElement
) {

    const response =
        await fetch(
            `${BACKEND_URL}/chat-stream-test`,
            {
                method: "POST",

                headers: {
                    "Content-Type":
                        "application/json"
                },

                body: JSON.stringify({
                    message:
                        userMessage
                })
            }
        );

    if (!response.ok) {

        throw new Error(
            `Servidor respondió ${response.status}`
        );

    }

    if (!response.body) {

        throw new Error(
            "El navegador no soporta streaming."
        );

    }

    /*
       Creamos una zona independiente
       para el texto.

       La animación queda intacta.
    */

    const responseText =
        document.createElement("div");

    responseText.className =
        "jonis-ai-stream-text";

    messageElement.appendChild(
        responseText
    );

    const reader =
        response.body.getReader();

    const decoder =
        new TextDecoder();

    let fullText = "";

    while (true) {

        const {
            value,
            done
        } =
            await reader.read();

        if (done) {

            break;

        }

        fullText +=
            decoder.decode(
                value,
                {
                    stream: true
                }
            );

        /*
           SOLO actualizamos el texto.

           La animación no se toca.
        */

        responseText.innerHTML =
            formatResponse(
                fullText
            );

        scrollToBottom();

    }

    return fullText;
}

// ==========================================
// ENVIAR MENSAJE
// ==========================================

async function sendMessage() {

if (
    isSending
) {

    return;

}


const userMessage =
    userInput.value.trim();


const imageToSend =
    selectedImage;


if (

    !userMessage &&

    !imageToSend

) {

    userInput.focus();

    return;

}


isSending =
    true;


sendBtn.disabled =
    true;


sendBtn.textContent =
    "Enviando...";


// ======================================
// MOSTRAR MENSAJE DEL USUARIO
// ======================================

if (
    userMessage
) {

    addMessage(
        "user",
        userMessage
    );

}


if (
    imageToSend
) {

    addImageMessage(
        imageToSend
    );

}


userInput.value =
    "";

userInput.style.height =
    "auto";


const thinkingMessage =
    addMessage(

        "assistant",

        `
        <div class="jonis-ai-processing">

            <div class="jonis-ai-orbit orbit-1"></div>
            <div class="jonis-ai-orbit orbit-2"></div>
            <div class="jonis-ai-orbit orbit-3"></div>

            <div class="jonis-ai-particles">

                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>

            </div>

            <div class="jonis-ai-bot">

                <div class="jonis-ai-bot-glow"></div>

                <div class="jonis-ai-face">

                    <div class="jonis-ai-eye"></div>
                    <div class="jonis-ai-eye"></div>

                </div>

            </div>

            <div class="jonis-ai-brain">

                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>
                <span></span>

            </div>

            <div class="jonis-ai-name">
                JONIS AI
            </div>

            <div class="jonis-ai-status">

                Generando respuesta

                <span class="jonis-ai-dots">
                    <i>.</i>
                    <i>.</i>
                    <i>.</i>
                </span>

            </div>

        </div>
        `,

        true

    );


try {

    // ==================================
    // CONSULTAR IA
    // ==================================

    const response =
        await getResponse(

            userMessage,

            imageToSend

        );


    // ==================================
    // MOSTRAR RESPUESTA
    // ==================================

        // ==================================
    // EFECTO DE ESCRITURA JONIS AI
    // ==================================

    thinkingMessage.innerHTML = "";

    const responseContainer =
        document.createElement("div");

    responseContainer.className =
        "jonis-ai-typing-response";

    thinkingMessage.appendChild(
        responseContainer
    );

    // Escribir rápidamente el texto
    let position = 0;

    // Velocidad de escritura
    const speed = 8;

    function writeResponse() {

        if (position >= response.length) {

            // Al terminar, aplicar el formato completo
            responseContainer.innerHTML =
                formatResponse(response);

            setupCopyButtons(
                thinkingMessage
            );

            scrollToBottom();

            return;
        }

        // Escribir varios caracteres por ciclo
        const charsPerStep = 3;

        position += charsPerStep;

        if (position > response.length) {
            position = response.length;
        }

        // Mostrar temporalmente el texto
        responseContainer.textContent =
            response.substring(
                0,
                position
            );

        scrollToBottom();

        setTimeout(
            writeResponse,
            speed
        );
    }

    writeResponse();


    // ==================================
    // GUARDAR CONVERSACIÓN EN MEMORIA
    // ==================================

    if (
        userMessage
    ) {

        conversationHistory.push({

            role:
                "user",

            content:
                userMessage

        });

    }


    conversationHistory.push({

        role:
            "assistant",

        content:
            response

    });

    localStorage.setItem(
        "jonisai_history",
        JSON.stringify(
            conversationHistory
        
        )
    );

    // ==================================
    // LIMITAR MEMORIA DEL NAVEGADOR
    // ==================================

    if (
        conversationHistory.length > 20
    ) {

        conversationHistory =
            conversationHistory.slice(
                -20
            );

    }


    console.log(
        "Memoria actualizada:",
        conversationHistory
    );
    
    // ==================================
    // ACTUALIZAR LISTA DE CHATS
    // ==================================

    await loadConversations();

    scrollToBottom();

}


catch (error) {

    console.error(
        "ERROR:",
        error
    );


    thinkingMessage.textContent =
        "❌ Error: " +
        error.message;

}


finally {

    // ==================================
    // LIMPIAR IMAGEN
    // ==================================

    selectedImage =
        null;


    imageInput.value =
        "";


    imagePreview.src =
        "";


    imagePreviewContainer.style.display =
        "none";


    // ==================================
    // LIMPIAR ARCHIVO
    // ==================================

    selectedFile =
        null;


    fileInput.value =
        "";


    fileName.textContent =
        "";


    filePreview.style.display =
        "none";


    // ==================================
    // REACTIVAR
    // ==================================

    isSending =
        false;


    sendBtn.disabled =
        false;


    sendBtn.textContent =
        "Enviar";


    userInput.focus();


    scrollToBottom();

}

}

// ==========================================
// BOTÓN ENVIAR

sendBtn.addEventListener(

function() {

    sendMessage();

}

);

    ) {

        event.preventDefault();

        sendMessage();

    }

}

);

    userInput.style.height =
        userInput.scrollHeight +
        "px";

}

);

// ==========================================
// CARGAR HISTORIAL GUARDADO
// ==========================================

function loadSavedHistory() {

    if (
        conversationHistory.length === 0
    ) {

        return;

    }

    // Limpiar el mensaje inicial
    messagesDiv.innerHTML = "";

    // Mostrar mensajes guardados
    conversationHistory.forEach(
        function(item) {

            if (
                item.role === "user" ||
                item.role === "assistant"
            ) {

                addMessage(
                    item.role,
                    item.content
                );

            }

        }
    );

}


// ==========================================
// INICIAR JONISAI
// ==========================================

// ==========================================
// INICIALIZAR JONISAI ULTRA
// ==========================================

async function initializeUltra() {

    try {

        let deviceId = localStorage.getItem(
            "jonisai_ultra_device_id"
        );

        if (!deviceId) {

            deviceId =
                "jonis-" +
                crypto.randomUUID();

            localStorage.setItem(
                "jonisai_ultra_device_id",
                deviceId
            );

            console.log(
                "🆔 Nuevo dispositivo Ultra creado"
            );
        }

        const response = await fetch(
            BACKEND_URL + "/api/bootstrap",
            {
                method: "POST",

                headers: {
                    "Content-Type":
                        "application/json"
                },

                body: JSON.stringify({
                    device_id: deviceId
                })
            }
        );

        const data = await response.json();

        if (!response.ok || !data.ok) {

            console.error(
                "❌ Error inicializando Ultra:",
                data
            );

            return;
        }

        if (data.active) {

            localStorage.setItem(
                "jonisai_ultra_access_id",
                data.active.access_id
            );

            console.log(
                "🪙 Ultra conectado:",
                data.active.account_id,
                "| Tokens:",
                data.active.tokens
            );
        }

    } catch (error) {

        console.error(
            "❌ No se pudo inicializar Ultra:",
            error
        );
    }
}


async function refreshUltraAccount() {
    const status = document.getElementById("ultra-status");
    const accountEl = document.getElementById("ultra-account");
    const tokensEl = document.getElementById("ultra-tokens");
    const message = document.getElementById("ultra-message");

    try {
        const deviceId = localStorage.getItem("jonisai_ultra_device_id");
        const accessId = localStorage.getItem("jonisai_ultra_access_id");

        if (!deviceId || !accessId) {
            throw new Error("No se encontró la sesión Ultra.");
        }

        const response = await fetch(`${BACKEND_URL}/api/me`, {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify({
                device_id: deviceId,
                access_id: accessId
            })
        });

        const data = await response.json();

        if (!response.ok || !data.ok || !data.account) {
            throw new Error(data.error || "No se pudo consultar la cuenta.");
        }

        status.textContent = "Conectada";
        accountEl.textContent =
            data.account.account_number ?? data.account.account_id;
        tokensEl.textContent =
            Number(data.account.tokens ?? 0).toLocaleString("es-NI");
        message.textContent = "Saldo actualizado.";
    } catch (error) {
        status.textContent = "Sin conexión";
        message.textContent = error.message;
    }
}

async function loadUltraPackages() {
    const container = document.getElementById("ultra-packages");

    try {
        const response = await fetch(`${BACKEND_URL}/api/packages`);
        const data = await response.json();

        if (!response.ok || !data.ok || !Array.isArray(data.packages)) {
            throw new Error("No se pudieron cargar los paquetes.");
        }

        container.replaceChildren();

        data.packages.forEach(pkg => {
            const item = document.createElement("div");
            item.className = "ultra-package";

            const name = document.createElement("strong");
            name.textContent = pkg.name;

            const price = document.createElement("p");
            price.textContent =
                `🇳🇮 ${pkg.price} · 🇺🇸 US$${pkg.paypal_amount} USD`;

            const button = document.createElement("button");
            button.type = "button";
            button.textContent = "💳 Comprar con PayPal";

            button.addEventListener("click", async () => {
                const deviceId = localStorage.getItem(
                    "jonisai_ultra_device_id"
                );
                const accessId = localStorage.getItem(
                    "jonisai_ultra_access_id"
                );
                const message = document.getElementById("ultra-message");

                if (!deviceId || !accessId) {
                    message.textContent = "Primero conecta tu cuenta Ultra.";
                    return;
                }

                button.disabled = true;
                message.textContent = "Preparando compra...";

                try {
                    const response = await fetch(
                        `${BACKEND_URL}/api/paypal/create-order`,
                        {
                            method: "POST",
                            headers: {
                                "Content-Type": "application/json"
                            },
                            body: JSON.stringify({
                                package_id: pkg.id,
                                device_id: deviceId,
                                access_id: accessId
                            })
                        }
                    );

                    const data = await response.json();

                    if (!response.ok || !data.ok) {
                        throw new Error(
                            data.error || "No se pudo crear la compra."
                        );
                    }

                    const approval = (data.links || []).find(
                        link => link.rel === "approve"
                    );

                    if (!approval || !approval.href) {
                        throw new Error(
                            "PayPal no devolvió el enlace de aprobación."
                        );
                    }

                    window.location.href = approval.href;
                } catch (error) {
                    message.textContent = error.message;
                    button.disabled = false;
                }
            });

            item.append(name, price, button);
            container.appendChild(item);
        });
    } catch (error) {
        container.textContent = error.message;
    }
}

const ultraRefreshButton = document.getElementById("ultra-refresh");

if (ultraRefreshButton) {
    ultraRefreshButton.addEventListener("click", refreshUltraAccount);
}

async function initializeJonisAI() {

    console.log(
        "🤖 JonisAI cargado correctamente"
    );

    await initializeUltra();
    await handlePayPalReturn();
    await refreshUltraAccount();
    await loadUltraPackages();
    
    console.log(
        "🧠 Memoria de conversación activada"
    );

    await loadModels();

    await loadConversations();

    // Recuperar la última conversación guardada

    if (currentConversationId) {

        try {

            await openConversation(
                Number(currentConversationId)
            );

        } catch (error) {

            console.log(
                "⚠️ La conversación guardada ya no existe. Creando una nueva..."
            );

            localStorage.removeItem(
                "jonisai_conversation_id"
            );

            localStorage.removeItem(
                "jonisai_history"
            );

        }

    }

}

async function handlePayPalReturn() {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const orderId = params.get("token");
    const result = params.get("paypal");
    const message = document.getElementById("ultra-message");

    if (result === "cancel") {
        message.textContent = "Compra cancelada. No se agregaron tokens.";
    }

    if (orderId) {
        const deviceId = localStorage.getItem(
            "jonisai_ultra_device_id"
        );
        const accessId = localStorage.getItem(
            "jonisai_ultra_access_id"
        );

        if (!deviceId || !accessId) {
            message.textContent =
                "No se encontró la cuenta Ultra para confirmar el pago.";
            return;
        }

        message.textContent = "Verificando el pago con PayPal...";

        try {
            const response = await fetch(
                `${BACKEND_URL}/api/paypal/capture-order`,
                {
                    method: "POST",
                    headers: {
                        "Content-Type": "application/json"
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        order_id: orderId,
                        device_id: deviceId,
                        access_id: accessId
                    })
                }
            );

            const data = await response.json();

            if (!response.ok || !data.ok) {
                throw new Error(
                    data.error || "No se pudo confirmar el pago."
                );
            }

            message.textContent =
                "¡Pago confirmado! Actualizando tus tokens...";
        } catch (error) {
            message.textContent =
                "No pudimos confirmar el pago: " + error.message;
        }
    }

    if (result || orderId) {
        window.history.replaceState(
            {},
            document.title,
            window.location.pathname
        );
    }
}

initializeJonisAI();

</script></body>
</html>

// ==========================================
// URL DEL BACKEND
// ==========================================

const BACKEND_URL =
    "https://jonis-backend.onrender.com";


// ==========================================
// ELEMENTOS
// ==========================================

const messagesDiv =
    document.getElementById("messages");

const userInput =
    document.getElementById("user-input");

const sendBtn =
    document.getElementById("send-btn");

const imageInput =
    document.getElementById("image-input");

const imagePreview =
    document.getElementById("image-preview");

const imagePreviewContainer =
    document.getElementById(
        "image-preview-container"
    );

const removeImageBtn =
    document.getElementById(
        "remove-image"
    );


// ==========================================
// IMAGEN SELECCIONADA
// ==========================================

let selectedImage = null;


// ==========================================
// MOSTRAR MENSAJE
// ==========================================

function addMessage(role, content) {

    const messageDiv =
        document.createElement("div");

    messageDiv.classList.add(
        "message",
        role
    );

    messageDiv.textContent =
        content;

    messagesDiv.appendChild(
        messageDiv
    );

    messagesDiv.scrollTop =
        messagesDiv.scrollHeight;

    return messageDiv;
}


// ==========================================
// SELECCIONAR IMAGEN
// ==========================================

imageInput.addEventListener(
    "change",
    function() {

        const file =
            imageInput.files[0];

        if (!file) {
            return;
        }


        // Comprobar que sea imagen

        if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) {

            alert(
                "Selecciona una imagen válida."
            );

            imageInput.value = "";

            return;
        }


        // Leer imagen

        const reader =
            new FileReader();


        reader.onload = function(event) {

            selectedImage =
                event.target.result;


            imagePreview.src =
                selectedImage;


            imagePreviewContainer.style.display =
                "block";

        };


        reader.readAsDataURL(file);

    }
);


// ==========================================
// QUITAR IMAGEN
// ==========================================

removeImageBtn.addEventListener(
    "click",
    function() {

        selectedImage = null;

        imageInput.value = "";

        imagePreview.src = "";

        imagePreviewContainer.style.display =
            "none";

    }
);


// ==========================================
// CONSULTAR BACKEND
// ==========================================

async function getResponse(
    userMessage,
    image
) {

    const url =
        `${BACKEND_URL}/chat`;


    console.log(
        "Enviando mensaje:",
        userMessage
    );


    console.log(
        "Tiene imagen:",
        Boolean(image)
    );


    try {

        const response =
            await fetch(
                url,
                {

                    method: "POST",

                    headers: {
                        "Content-Type":
                            "application/json"
                    },

                    body: JSON.stringify({

                        message:
                            userMessage,

                        image:
                            image

                    })

                }
            );


        if (!response.ok) {

            throw new Error(
                `Servidor respondió ${response.status}`
            );

        }


        const data =
            await response.json();


        if (data.response) {

            return data.response;

        }


        if (data.error) {

            return (
                "Error del backend: "
                + data.error
            );

        }


        return (
            "El servidor no devolvió "
            + "una respuesta válida."
        );

    }

    catch (error) {

        console.error(
            "ERROR:",
            error
        );


        return (
            "Error de conexión con el servidor: "
            + error.message
        );

    }

}


// ==========================================
// ENVIAR MENSAJE
// ==========================================

async function sendMessage() {

    const userMessage =
        userInput.value.trim();


    // Debe existir texto o imagen

    if (
        !userMessage &&
        !selectedImage
    ) {

        return;

    }


    // ======================================
    // MOSTRAR MENSAJE DEL USUARIO
    // ======================================

    let displayMessage =
        userMessage;


    if (selectedImage) {

        displayMessage +=
            "\n📷 Imagen adjunta";

    }


    addMessage(
        "user",
        displayMessage
    );


    // ======================================
    // LIMPIAR TEXTO
    // ======================================

    userInput.value = "";


    // ======================================
    // PENSANDO
    // ======================================

    const thinkingMessage =
        addMessage(
            "assistant",
            "Pensando..."
        );


    sendBtn.disabled = true;


    // ======================================
    // GUARDAR IMAGEN ACTUAL
    // ======================================

    const imageToSend =
        selectedImage;


    // ======================================
    // OBTENER RESPUESTA
    // ======================================

    const response =
        await getResponse(
            userMessage,
            imageToSend
        );


    thinkingMessage.textContent =
        response;


    // ======================================
    // LIMPIAR IMAGEN
    // ======================================

    selectedImage = null;

    imageInput.value = "";

    imagePreview.src = "";

    imagePreviewContainer.style.display =
        "none";


    // ======================================
    // ACTIVAR BOTÓN
    // ======================================

    sendBtn.disabled = false;

    userInput.focus();

}


// ==========================================
// BOTÓN ENVIAR
// ==========================================

sendBtn.addEventListener(
    "click",
    sendMessage
);


// ==========================================
// ENTER
// ==========================================

userInput.addEventListener(
    "keydown",
    function(event) {

        if (
            event.key === "Enter" &&
            !event.shiftKey
        ) {

            event.preventDefault();

            sendMessage();

        }

    }
);

</script></body></html>

// ==========================================
// CONFIGURACIÓN
// ==========================================

const BACKEND_URL =
    "https://jonis-backend.onrender.com";


// ==========================================
// ELEMENTOS HTML
// ==========================================

const messagesDiv =
    document.getElementById("messages");

const userInput =
    document.getElementById("user-input");

const sendBtn =
    document.getElementById("send-btn");

const imageInput =
    document.getElementById("image-input");

const imagePreview =
    document.getElementById("image-preview");

const imagePreviewContainer =
    document.getElementById(
        "image-preview-container"
    );

const removeImageBtn =
    document.getElementById("remove-image");

const fileInput =
    document.getElementById("file-input");

const modelSelect =
    document.getElementById("model-select");

// ==========================================
// GUARDAR MODELO SELECCIONADO
// ==========================================

const savedModel =
    localStorage.getItem("jonisai_model");

if (savedModel) {
    modelSelect.value = savedModel;
}

modelSelect.addEventListener(
    "change",
    function() {

        localStorage.setItem(
            "jonisai_model",
            modelSelect.value
        );

    }
);

// ==========================================
// CARGAR MODELOS
// ==========================================

async function loadModels() {

    try {

        const response =
            await fetch(
                `${BACKEND_URL}/models`
            );

        const data =
            await response.json();

        modelSelect.innerHTML = "";

        data.models.forEach(function(model) {

            const option =
                document.createElement("option");

            option.value =
                model.key;

            option.textContent =
                model.name;

            if (
                model.key === data.default
            ) {

                option.selected = true;

            }

            modelSelect.appendChild(option);

        });

    }

    catch (error) {

        console.error(
            "Error cargando modelos:",
            error
        );

        modelSelect.innerHTML =
            '<option value="">❌ Error cargando modelos</option>';

    }

}

const filePreview =
    document.getElementById("file-preview");

const fileName =
    document.getElementById("file-name");

const removeFileBtn =
    document.getElementById("remove-file");


// ==========================================
// ESTADO
// ==========================================

let selectedImage = null;

let selectedFile = null;

let isSending = false;

// ==========================================
// SISTEMA DE CHATS
// ==========================================

const sidebar =
    document.getElementById("sidebar");

const openSidebarBtn =
    document.getElementById("open-sidebar-btn");

const closeSidebarBtn =
    document.getElementById("close-sidebar-btn");

const newChatBtn =
    document.getElementById("new-chat-btn");

const conversationList =
    document.getElementById("conversation-list");


// ==========================================
// ABRIR / CERRAR SIDEBAR
// ==========================================

if (openSidebarBtn) {

    openSidebarBtn.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function() {

            sidebar.classList.add(
                "sidebar-open"
            );

        }
    );

}


if (closeSidebarBtn) {

    closeSidebarBtn.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function() {

            sidebar.classList.remove(
                "sidebar-open"
            );

        }
    );

}


// ==========================================
// CARGAR LISTA DE CONVERSACIONES
// ==========================================

async function loadConversations() {

    try {

        conversationList.innerHTML =
            '<div class="history-loading">Cargando chats...</div>';


        const response =
            await fetch(
                `${BACKEND_URL}/conversations`
            );


        const data =
            await response.json();


        if (!response.ok) {

            throw new Error(
                data.error ||
                "No se pudieron cargar los chats."
            );

        }


        conversationList.innerHTML = "";


        const conversations =
            data.conversations ||
            data ||
            [];


        if (
            conversations.length === 0
        ) {

            conversationList.innerHTML =
                '<div class="history-empty">No hay chats todavía.</div>';

            return;

        }


        conversations.forEach(
            function(conversation) {

                createConversationItem(
                    conversation
                );

            }
        );


    }

    catch (error) {

        console.error(
            "Error cargando conversaciones:",
            error
        );


        conversationList.innerHTML =
    `<div class="history-error">❌ Error: ${error.message}</div>`;

    }

}


// ==========================================
// CREAR ELEMENTO DE CHAT
// ==========================================

function createConversationItem(
    conversation
) {

    const item =
        document.createElement(
            "div"
        );


    item.className =
        "conversation-item";

    const id =
        conversation.id;

    item.dataset.id =
        id;


    const title =
        conversation.title ||
        conversation.name ||
        `Chat ${id}`;


    item.innerHTML = `

        <button
            type="button"
            class="conversation-button"
        >

            <span class="conversation-icon">
                💬
            </span>

            <span class="conversation-title">
                ${escapeHtml(title)}
            </span>

        </button>

        <button
            type="button"
            class="delete-conversation-btn"
            title="Eliminar chat"
        >
            🗑️
        </button>

    `;


    const conversationButton =
        item.querySelector(
            ".conversation-button"
        );


    const deleteButton =
        item.querySelector(
            ".delete-conversation-btn"
        );


    // Abrir conversación

    conversationButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function() {

            openConversation(
                id
            );

        }
    );


    // Eliminar conversación

    deleteButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        function(event) {

            event.stopPropagation();

            deleteConversation(
                id
            );

        }
    );


    if (
        String(id) ===
        String(currentConversationId)
    ) {

        item.classList.add(
            "active"
        );

    }


    conversationList.appendChild(
        item
    );

}


// ==========================================
// ABRIR CHAT
// ==========================================

async function openConversation(
    conversationId
) {

    try {

        const response =
            await fetch(
                `${BACKEND_URL}/conversations/${conversationId}`
            );


        const data =
            await response.json();


        if (!response.ok) {

            throw new Error(
                data.error ||
                "La conversación no existe."
            );

        }


        currentConversationId =
            conversationId;


        localStorage.setItem(
            "jonisai_conversation_id",
            conversationId
        );


        messagesDiv.innerHTML = "";


        const messages =
            data.messages ||
            data.conversation?.messages ||
            [];


        messages.forEach(
            function(message) {

                if (
                    message.role === "user" ||
                    message.role === "assistant"
                ) {

                    addMessage(
                        message.role,
                        message.content
                    );

                }

            }
        );


        conversationHistory =
            messages.filter(
                function(message) {

                    return (
                        message.role === "user" ||
                        message.role === "assistant"
                    );

                }
            ).map(
                function(message) {

                    return {

                        role:
                            message.role,

                        content:
                            message.content

                    };

                }
            );


        localStorage.setItem(
            "jonisai_history",
            JSON.stringify(
                conversationHistory
            )
        );


        document
            .querySelectorAll(
                ".conversation-item"
            )
            .forEach(
                function(item) {

                    item.classList.remove(
                        "active"
                    );

                }
            );


        const activeItem =
            Array.from(
                document.querySelectorAll(
                    ".conversation-item"
                )
            ).find(
                function(item) {

                    const button =
                        item.querySelector(
                            ".conversation-button"
                        );

                    return button &&
                        item.dataset.id ===
                        String(conversationId);

                }
            );


        if (activeItem) {

            activeItem.classList.add(
                "active"
            );

        }


        // En teléfono cerramos el menú

        sidebar.classList.remove(
            "sidebar-open"
        );


        scrollToBottom();


    }

    catch (error) {

        console.error(
            "Error abriendo conversación:",
            error
        );


        alert(
            "❌ " +
            error.message
        );

    }

}


// ==========================================
// ELIMINAR CHAT
// ==========================================

async function deleteConversation(
    conversationId
) {

    const confirmed =
        confirm(
            "¿Quieres eliminar este chat?"
        );


    if (!confirmed) {

        return;

    }


    try {

        const response =
            await fetch(
                `${BACKEND_URL}/conversations/${conversationId}`,
                {
                    method:
                        "DELETE"
                }
            );


        const data =
            await response.json();


        if (!response.ok) {

            throw new Error(
                data.error ||
                "No se pudo eliminar el chat."
            );

        }


        if (
            String(conversationId) ===
            String(currentConversationId)
        ) {

            currentConversationId =
                null;


            conversationHistory =
                [];


            localStorage.removeItem(
                "jonisai_conversation_id"
            );


            localStorage.removeItem(
                "jonisai_history"
            );


            messagesDiv.innerHTML = "";


            showWelcomeMessage();

        }


        await loadConversations();


    }

    catch (error) {

        console.error(
            "Error eliminando conversación:",
            error
        );


        alert(
            "❌ " +
            error.message
        );

    }

}


// ==========================================
// NUEVO CHAT
// ==========================================

async function createNewChat() {

    try {

        const response =
            await fetch(
                `${BACKEND_URL}/conversations`,
                {
                    method:
                        "POST",

                    headers: {
                        "Content-Type":
                            "application/json"
                    },

                    body:
                        JSON.stringify({
                            model:
                                modelSelect.value
                        })
                }
            );


        const data =
            await response.json();


        if (!response.ok) {

            throw new Error(
                data.error ||
                "No se pudo crear el chat."
            );

        }


        const newId =
            data.id ||
            data.conversation_id ||
            data.conversation?.id;


        if (!newId) {

            throw new Error(
                "El servidor no devolvió el ID del nuevo chat."
            );

        }


        currentConversationId =
            newId;


        conversationHistory =
            [];


        localStorage.setItem(
            "jonisai_conversation_id",
            newId
        );


        localStorage.setItem(
            "jonisai_history",
            JSON.stringify([])
        );


        messagesDiv.innerHTML = "";


        showWelcomeMessage();


        await loadConversations();


        sidebar.classList.remove(
            "sidebar-open"
        );


        userInput.focus();


    }

    catch (error) {

        console.error(
            "Error creando chat:",
            error
        );


        alert(
            "❌ " +
            error.message
        );

    }

}


// ==========================================
// MENSAJE DE BIENVENIDA
// ==========================================

function showWelcomeMessage() {

    addMessage(
        "assistant",
        `¡Hola! 👋 Soy JonisAI 🤖

¿Qué quieres hacer el día de hoy?

Puedo ayudarte con preguntas, ideas, aprendizaje, programación, textos, problemas y proyectos.

Puedes escribirme lo que necesites y te ayudaré paso a paso.`
    );

}


// ==========================================
// BOTÓN NUEVO CHAT
// ==========================================

if (newChatBtn) {

    newChatBtn.addEventListener(
        "click",
        createNewChat
    );

}

// ==========================================
// MEMORIA DE LA CONVERSACIÓN
// ==========================================
//
// Aquí guardamos los mensajes que ya
// tuvieron respuesta.
//
// Ejemplo:
//
// {
//     role: "user",
//     content: "hola"
// }
//
// {
//     role: "assistant",
//     content: "¡Hola!"
// }
//
// Luego enviamos esta información
// al backend como "history".
//

let conversationHistory =
    JSON.parse(
        localStorage.getItem(
            "jonisai_history"
        )
    ) || [];
let currentConversationId =
    localStorage.getItem(
        "jonisai_conversation_id"
    );


// ==========================================
// SCROLL
// ==========================================

function scrollToBottom() {

    messagesDiv.scrollTo({

        top:
            messagesDiv.scrollHeight,

        behavior:
            "smooth"

    });

}


// ==========================================
// ESCAPAR HTML
// ==========================================

function escapeHtml(text) {

    const div =
        document.createElement("div");

    div.textContent =
        text;

    return div.innerHTML;

}


// ==========================================
// CONVERTIR URLS EN ENLACES
// ==========================================

function linkify(text) {

    const urlRegex =
        /(https?:\/\/[^\s<]+)/g;

    return text.replace(

        urlRegex,

        function(url) {

            return `<a
                href="${url}"
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                class="chat-link"
            >${url}</a>`;

        }

    );

}


// ==========================================
// FORMATEAR TEXTO
// ==========================================

function formatText(text) {

    let safeText =
        escapeHtml(
            String(text || "")
        );

    safeText =
        linkify(
            safeText
        );

    safeText =
        safeText.replace(
            /\n/g,
            "<br>"
        );

    return safeText;

}


// ==========================================
// FORMATEAR RESPUESTA
// ==========================================

function formatResponse(text) {
    const originalText = String(text || "");

    const codeRegex = /```([a-zA-Z0-9_+#.-]*)\n?([\s\S]*?)```/g;

    function formatInline(value) {
        let html = escapeHtml(value);

        html = html.replace(
            /`([^`\n]+)`/g,
            '<span class="inline-code">$1</span>'
        );

        html = html.replace(
            /\*\*([^*\n]+)\*\*/g,
            "<strong>$1</strong>"
        );

        html = html.replace(
            /(https?:\/\/[^\s<]+)/g,
            function(url) {
                return `<a href="${url}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="chat-link">${url}</a>`;
            }
        );

        return html;
    }

    function formatNormalText(value) {
        const lines = value.split("\n");

        let html = "";
        let paragraph = [];
        let unorderedList = false;
        let orderedList = false;

        function closeLists() {
            if (unorderedList) {
                html += "</ul>";
                unorderedList = false;
            }

            if (orderedList) {
                html += "</ol>";
                orderedList = false;
            }
        }

        function closeParagraph() {
            if (paragraph.length > 0) {
                const content = paragraph.join(" ").trim();

                if (content) {
                    html += `<div class="response-paragraph">${formatInline(content)}</div>`;
                }

                paragraph = [];
            }
        }

        for (const line of lines) {
            const trimmed = line.trim();

            if (!trimmed) {
                closeParagraph();
                closeLists();
                continue;
            }

            const heading = trimmed.match(/^#{1,3}\s+(.+)$/);

            if (heading) {
                closeParagraph();
                closeLists();

                html += `<div class="response-heading">${formatInline(heading[1])}</div>`;

                continue;
            }

            const unordered = trimmed.match(/^[-*]\s+(.+)$/);

            if (unordered) {
                closeParagraph();

                if (orderedList) {
                    html += "</ol>";
                    orderedList = false;
                }

                if (!unorderedList) {
                    html += '<ul class="response-list">';
                    unorderedList = true;
                }

                html += `<li>${formatInline(unordered[1])}</li>`;

                continue;
            }

            const ordered = trimmed.match(/^\d+[.)]\s+(.+)$/);

            if (ordered) {
                closeParagraph();

                if (unorderedList) {
                    html += "</ul>";
                    unorderedList = false;
                }

                if (!orderedList) {
                    html += '<ol class="response-list response-list-ordered">';
                    orderedList = true;
                }

                html += `<li>${formatInline(ordered[1])}</li>`;

                continue;
            }

            closeLists();

            paragraph.push(trimmed);
        }

        closeParagraph();
        closeLists();

        return html;
    }

    let result = "";
    let lastIndex = 0;
    let match;

    while ((match = codeRegex.exec(originalText)) !== null) {
        const textBefore = originalText.slice(lastIndex, match.index);

        result += formatNormalText(textBefore);

        const language = match[1] || "Código";
        const code = escapeHtml(match[2]);

        result += `
<div class="code-block">
    <div class="code-header">
        <span>${escapeHtml(language)}</span>
        <button class="copy-code-btn" type="button">📋 Copiar</button>
    </div>
    <pre><code>${code}</code></pre>
</div>`;

        lastIndex = codeRegex.lastIndex;
    }

    result += formatNormalText(
        originalText.slice(lastIndex)
    );

    return result;
}

// ==========================================
// COPIAR TEXTO
// ==========================================

async function copyText(text) {

if (

    navigator.clipboard &&

    window.isSecureContext

) {

    await navigator
        .clipboard
        .writeText(
            text
        );

    return;

}


const textarea =
    document.createElement(
        "textarea"
    );

textarea.value =
    text;

textarea.style.position =
    "fixed";

textarea.style.opacity =
    "0";

document.body.appendChild(
    textarea
);

textarea.select();

document.execCommand(
    "copy"
);

document.body.removeChild(
    textarea
);

}

// ==========================================
// BOTONES COPIAR
// ==========================================

function setupCopyButtons(container) {

const copyButtons =
    container.querySelectorAll(
        ".copy-code-btn"
    );


copyButtons.forEach(

    function(button) {

        button.addEventListener(

            "click",

            async function() {

                const codeBlock =
                    button.closest(
                        ".code-block"
                    );


                const codeElement =
                    codeBlock.querySelector(
                        "code"
                    );


                const code =
                    codeElement.textContent;


                const oldText =
                    button.textContent;


                try {

                    await copyText(
                        code
                    );


                    button.textContent =
                        "✓ Copiado";


                    setTimeout(

                        function() {

                            button.textContent =
                                oldText;

                        },

                        2000

                    );

                }

                catch (error) {

                    console.error(
                        "Error al copiar:",
                        error
                    );


                    button.textContent =
                        "❌ Error";


                    setTimeout(

                        function() {

                            button.textContent =
                                oldText;

                        },

                        2000

                    );

                }

            }

        );

    }

);

}

// ==========================================
// MOSTRAR MENSAJE
// ==========================================

function addMessage(
    role,
    content,
    rawHTML = false
) {

    const messageDiv =
        document.createElement(
            "div"
        );


    messageDiv.classList.add(
        "message",
        role
    );


    if (
        role === "assistant"
    ) {

        if (rawHTML) {

            messageDiv.innerHTML =
                content;

        }

        else {

            messageDiv.innerHTML =
                formatResponse(
                    content
                );

            setupCopyButtons(
                messageDiv
            );

        }

    }

    else {

        messageDiv.textContent =
            content;

    }


    messagesDiv.appendChild(
        messageDiv
    );


    scrollToBottom();


    return messageDiv;

}

// ==========================================
// MOSTRAR IMAGEN
// ==========================================

function addImageMessage(
imageData
) {

const imageMessage =
    document.createElement(
        "div"
    );


imageMessage.classList.add(
    "message",
    "user"
);


const image =
    document.createElement(
        "img"
    );


image.src =
    imageData;


image.alt =
    "Imagen enviada";


image.classList.add(
    "chat-image"
);


imageMessage.appendChild(
    image
);


messagesDiv.appendChild(
    imageMessage
);


scrollToBottom();

}

// ==========================================
// SELECCIONAR IMAGEN
// ==========================================

imageInput.addEventListener(

"change",

function() {

    const file =
        imageInput.files[0];


    if (!file) {

        return;

    }


    if (

        !file.type.startsWith(
            "image/"
        )

    ) {

        alert(
            "Selecciona una imagen válida."
        );


        imageInput.value =
            "";


        return;

    }


    const reader =
        new FileReader();


    reader.onload =
        function(event) {

            selectedImage =
                event.target.result;


            imagePreview.src =
                selectedImage;


            imagePreviewContainer.style.display =
                "flex";

        };


    reader.onerror =
        function() {

            alert(
                "No se pudo leer la imagen."
            );

        };


    reader.readAsDataURL(
        file
    );

}

);

    selectedImage =
